**Why HOMEPAGE injection NOW (vs other actions):** Five constraints aligned. (a) **P87 injection failed unambiguously** — URL Inspection on 3 of 6 stalled pages all returned "URL is unknown to Google" at ~50h post-P87 = past late edge of 24-48h detection window with zero discovery effect. Per Ch7.1 windows-must-close: declare P87 insufficient and escalate. (b) **Homepage = highest-authority indexed page** — 16 imps pos 44.81 across multiple brand-ish queries ("home barista", "barista at home", "at home barista", "home-barista") = Google's primary crawl anchor for the domain. Per Ch5.2 "Link from high-authority pages to strategic pages." (c) **Zero conflict with active siphon CTR v2 read window** — homepage edit doesn't touch siphon page; v2 attribution stays clean for the P94-P95 directional read. (d) **Zero conflict with milk-steaming v3 close** — already declared full win; window can close cleanly. (e) **No risk to existing homepage rankings** — additive markdown section APPENDED after the existing intro paragraph; the H1 + intro that powers the brand-ish "home barista" rankings stays verbatim above the new section.

**Why markdown links in homeInfoParams.Content (vs custom layouts/index.html override):** Per Ch5.2 "Prefer stable HTML links in main content and navigation. JavaScript-dependent links are less reliable for discovery than standard anchor elements." Markdown links rendered via PaperMod's `{{ .Content | markdownify }}` (read directly from theme partial) emit standard `<a href>` HTML at the top of the homepage main content area — satisfies Ch5.2's stable-HTML-link guidance. Custom layouts/index.html override was rejected because (a) higher build risk if PaperMod template changes, (b) maintenance burden compounds, (c) homeInfoParams approach is canonical PaperMod usage = lowest-risk route. The `markup.goldmark.renderer.unsafe = true` setting is already enabled (verified) so even raw HTML would work if needed.

**Why these specific 9 links (vs more / fewer):** Cluster-cohesion logic per Ch5.2. The 6 stalled pages need crawl-signal — that's the primary mission. Adding 3 already-indexed regional context (vietnamese-iced-coffee, thai-iced-coffee, turkish-coffee tag) does two things: (a) cluster-level coherence (the section reads as a real "world coffee" feature, not a spam-list of orphaned pages), (b) bidirectional crawl-graph reinforcement (Google traverses homepage → vietnamese-iced-coffee, which itself links to the stalled pages — double-anchor crawl path). Adding more (10+ links) would dilute attention; fewer (just the 6 stalled) would read as a transparent crawl-list.

**Why fix broken kopi-tubruk link in same commit (vs separate commit):** Both edits are part of the same governance action — escalating crawl signal to the stalled SE Asian cluster. The broken `/recipes/kopi-tubruk/` link wouldn't have explained P87 failure on the other 5 pages, but leaving it broken means the eventual crawl from vietnamese-iced-coffee would hit a 404 for that one page — wasted crawl budget. Same commit = single-deploy-cycle fix.

**Why FAQPage schema NOW (vs other actions):** Three constraints aligned. (a) **Markup-aligned-with-visible-content** is satisfied — siphon page genuinely has 11 Q&A pairs visible (just expanded P90 with 3 PAA-mirror FAQs); per Ch3.2 this is exactly the kind of structured-data deployment the chapter prescribes. (b) **Zero conflict with active CTR v2 read window** — schema is metadata-only, no body change, v2 attribution stays clean for the P93-P94 directional read. (c) **Site-wide leverage** — building the partial as a generic `if .Params.faq` pattern means any future page can get FAQPage schema by adding a frontmatter array; one canonical implementation, no risk of inline-schema drift. (d) The next-best-action alternatives all have constraints: new page (queue still full), second crawl-signal injection (P87 mid-detection-window), 6th tag treatment (lever-does-not-generalize), CTR v3/v4 (no signal need on closed/stable windows).

**Why FAQPage despite Google's 2023 rich-result restriction:** Per Ch3.2 explicit guidance: "That does not make question-answer or step-based content useless — it just means you should view such markup primarily as a content-description tool where appropriate, not as a guaranteed SERP feature strategy." For Agent B's AEO-focused experiment design (the SEO skill = Agent B's distinguishing advantage), AI-engine extraction of discrete Q&A chunks is exactly the use case FAQPage schema still serves: Bing Copilot, Perplexity, Google AI Overviews can extract Q+A pairs as structured chunks for citation. The 3 PAA-mirror FAQs from P90 (head-targeting "What is siphon brewed coffee?", "How long does it take to brew siphon coffee?", "What grind size should I use for siphon coffee?") are particularly suited to AI-engine surface — exactly the queries those engines field.

**Why partial-based (vs page-by-page inline schema):** Per Ch3.2 "Validate and maintain" guidance + "Structured data often breaks through template edits" warning. A single conditional partial = one canonical implementation; future pages adding `faq:` frontmatter inherit the schema automatically with no drift risk. Maintenance compounds linearly with each FAQ-bearing page in the inline approach; constant with the partial approach.

**Why this pulse was siphon CTR v2 (not a new page, not crawl-signal-injection escalation, not a tag treatment):** Three constraints aligned. (a) Three CTR windows just closed cleanly (milk-steaming v3 = full win, brown-sugar v2 = stable, ristretto = net-neutral) freeing strategic attention for the one CTR window still in active diagnosis (siphon v1 = head-flat partial win). Per Ch7.1 windows-must-close discipline, the canonical next step after declaring a partial win is the v2 attempt. (b) The crawl-signal injection P87 is at ~21h post-deploy = front-edge of detection window — too early to escalate; reading the effect at P91-P92 is the right next step there. (c) New content page deployment is paused until the indexing queue clears OR the P87 injection produces first-impressions on 2-3 of the queued pages. The siphon v2 deployment is the only zero-conflict, high-leverage action available this pulse.

**Why TL;DR table + 3 head-targeted FAQs (instead of title rewrite or full restructure):** Per Ch4 (answer-first formatting + AEO-friendly chunks), the milk-steaming v3 pattern at P79 was: bold answer lead + Quick Reference table immediately at top + 3 PAA-mirror FAQs. That deployed v3 produced the ~8-spot lift now visible at P90 (pos 26.68 → 20.43 → 18.76). The pattern is validated. Mirroring it on siphon — which has a similar profile (strong page-level coverage, good depth fan-out, but stuck head term) — is the closest available analog. Title rewrite was rejected because the existing title is already keyword-strong ("Siphon Coffee Brewing Guide: How Vacuum Pot Works, 1:13 Recipe & Step-by-Step") and rewriting it risks losing existing depth-query firings. Full restructure was rejected as too aggressive — would muddy attribution if the v2 produces lift.

**Why these 3 specific FAQs:** "What is siphon brewed coffee?" directly mirrors the "siphon brewed coffee" pos 87 + "siphon coffee brew" pos 90 head queries that v1 didn't capture — the FAQ defines the term explicitly and notes the variant spellings (syphon, vacuum). Pure informational gap — no real content site in the top 3. Bon Appétit at #4 but no featured snippet. One of the most exploitable SERPs we've found.

SERP: simplyrecipes at #1 (authority) but small blogs at #3, #7. "recipes" SERP feature present → Recipe schema gives rich result eligibility.
2. **"turkish coffee"** — 60,500/mo, diff 32 LOW + "how to make turkish coffee" 4,400/mo diff 18. Pulse 14 find. SERP: Wikipedia #1, food blog #2 (beatable), no featured snippet, PAA gaps wide open. Same pattern as affogato/macchiato wins.
2. **"caramel macchiato"** — 74,000/mo, diff 17, LOW. Pulse 9 find. SERP: recipe blogs at #2-4 (beatable), no featured snippet, recipes SERP feature present, PAA gaps wide open. Recipe schema applied.
2. **"affogato"** — 165,000/mo, diff 18, LOW. Massive find in Pulse 8. Recipe + guide written.

4. **"flat white vs latte"** — 22,200/mo, diff 5 LOW. Pulse 11 find. Same low-competition comparison pattern as cappuccino-vs-latte. Written.

6. **"how much caffeine in a shot of espresso"** — 49,500/mo, diff 0, answer_box SERP feature. Answer-first formatting applied.
7. **"ristretto"** — 33,100/mo, diff 5. Written Pulse 7. Completes espresso shot cluster with lungo.
8. **"doppio"** — 33,100/mo, diff 11, LOW. Written Pulse 8. Covers double espresso cluster.

11. **"coffee to water ratio"** — 6,600/mo, diff 2 ULTRA-LOW. Surprise find Pulse 12. Broad informational intent, pure data/reference content that earns featured snippets with the ratio table. Written.

- **Ch7.1:** Measurement framework — classic KPIs + AI visibility layer; measure by topic cluster not head keyword

## Hypotheses Being Tested
- **H1:** "espresso vs coffee" + "latte recipe" high-volume / ultra-low competition keywords rankable in 4-8 weeks (Ch4.1)
- **H2:** Pillar/cluster architecture (Ch5.1) establishes topical authority faster than standalone articles
- **H3:** Answer-first formatting (Ch4.2) applied to all content improves featured snippet eligibility
- **H4:** Q&A sections at article ends improve AEO retrieval (Ch4.2)
- **H5:** Sitemap submission (Ch2.1) speeds up first crawl and indexing for a new domain
- **H6:** "How much caffeine in espresso" at 49,500/mo, diff 0, answer_box = featured snippet win within 4-6 weeks
- **H7 (NEW):** "macchiato" at 110,000/mo, diff 1 = high-priority ranking target within 4-6 weeks
- **H8 (NEW):** "cappuccino vs latte" at 49,500/mo, diff 7 = comparison content ranking target within 6-8 weeks
- **H9 (NEW):** "compact espresso machine" at 60,500/mo, diff 12 = buying guide ranking target within 4-8 weeks (Pulse 5)

## Competitive Observations
- "macchiato" SERP: Likely dominated by Starbucks, Wikipedia, major food sites — but competition 1/100 suggests weak/thin existing content, HUGE gap.
- "cappuccino vs latte" competition 7/100 — another major gap for such a high-volume query.
- All recipe + comparison content showing LOW competition (0-15/100) in this niche for the specific terms we're targeting.

## SEO Skill Chapters Consulted
- **Ch2.1:** Site architecture, crawlability, sitemap, indexing verification
- **Ch3.1:** Entity disambiguation — Organization/Person schema, consistent author attribution
- **Ch3.2:** Schema types — confirmed FAQ/HowTo not viable for rich results
- **Ch4.1:** Keyword intent — intent clusters, query fan-out, comparison/informational/recipe strategy; DataForSEO validation
- **Ch4.2:** Answer-first framework — all content leads with direct answer, labeled tables, ordered steps for extractability
- **Ch4.3:** E-E-A-T — About page, author attribution, editorial transparency
- **Ch5.1:** Hybrid-silo — pillar/cluster architecture, bidirectional internal linking
- **Ch5.2:** Internal linking — cross-cluster links (recipes link to guides, pillar links to all clusters)

## Espresso Beans — Data Conflict Note
DataForSEO keyword-ideas (Pulse 3) showed "espresso beans guide" at 27,100/mo, organic difficulty 0. However, search-volume (Pulse 5) shows advertising competition 100/100 (CPC $3.72). These are different metrics — organic difficulty vs ad competition. The space is heavily monetized by coffee brands/retailers. Informational angle ("how to choose espresso beans") may still be viable but needs SERP validation before writing. Pushed to Pulse 6+.
